Let's define a helper lemma for exactly this:
```
Lemma inst_open_tm_subst : forall sigma eta eta' x a t,
  (forall y, lc_tm (eta y)) ->
  (forall y, lc_tm (eta' y)) ->
  eta' x = a ->
  (forall y, In y (free_tm_vars t) -> eta' y = eta y) ->
  inst sigma eta' (open_tm t (tm_fvar x)) = open_tm (inst sigma eta t) a.
Proof.
  intros. unfold inst. rewrite tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm_rec.
  rewrite (tm_subst_env_open_tm_rec eta' H1 0 (tm_fvar x) (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t)).
  assert (Hx : tm_subst_env eta' (tm_fvar x) = a).
  { simpl. exact H3. }
  rewrite Hx.
  assert (Hag : tm_subst_env eta' (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t) = tm_subst_env eta (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t)).
  { apply tm_subst_env_agree. intros y Hy. apply H4.
    apply free_tm_vars_ty_subst. exact Hy. }
  rewrite Hag. reflexivity.
Qed.
```
But `inst sigma eta t` on the RHS equals tm_subst_env eta (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t); `unfold inst` in the goal unfolds both sides, so RHS becomes open_tm (tm_subst_env eta (...)) a. ✓.
Conditions: eta=eta1, eta'=eta1', x, a=a1, t=t2; H1=Heta1, H2=Heta1', eta' x = a1 (eta_update_self), and forall y free in t, eta' y = eta y (eta_update_other given y≠x and x∉free). 
Let's add this lemma. Similarly the T_TAbs analog:
```
Lemma inst_open_tm_ty_subst : forall sigma sigma' eta X U t,
  (forall Y, In Y (free_ty_vars_tm t) -> lc_ty (sigma' Y)) ->
  sigma' X = U ->
  (forall Y, In Y (free_ty_vars_tm t) -> sigma' Y = sigma Y) ->
  (forall y, lc_tm (eta y)) ->
  inst sigma' eta (open_tm_ty t (Ty_FVar X)) = open_tm_ty (inst sigma eta t) U.
Proof.
  intros. unfold inst.
  rewrite (tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm_ty_rec sigma' 0 (Ty_FVar X) t H).
  assert (HsX : ty_subst_env sigma' (Ty_FVar X) = U) by (simpl; exact H0).  (* sigma' X = U; ty_subst_env sigma' (FVar X) = sigma' X *)
  rewrite HsX.
  assert (Hag : tm_ty_subst_env sigma' t = tm_ty_subst_env sigma t).
  { apply tm_ty_subst_env_agree. intros Y HY. apply H1; exact HY. }
  rewrite Hag.
  rewrite (tm_subst_env_open_tm_ty_rec eta H3 0 U (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t)).
  reflexivity.
Qed.
```
Careful: after rewriting H and HsX and Hag, goal:

But the order of rewrites: after `rewrite (tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm_ty_rec ...)`, the goal is `tm_subst_env eta (open_tm_ty_rec 0 (ty_subst_env sigma' (Ty_FVar X)) (tm_ty_subst_env sigma' t)) = open_tm_ty (inst sigma eta t) U`. HsX rewrites; Hag rewrites. Then LHS = tm_subst_env eta (open_tm_ty_rec 0 U (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t)); RHS = open_tm_ty_rec 0 U (tm_subst_env eta (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t)) (after unfolding open_tm_ty on RHS? RHS was open_tm_ty (inst sigma eta t) U = open_ty? open_tm_ty is a Definition open_tm_ty_rec 0 U t; unfolding inst only on the RHS? `unfold inst` unfolds all occurrences of inst in the goal, including RHS, giving open_tm_ty (tm_subst_env eta (tm_ty_subst_env sigma t)) U. open_tm_ty is still folded; rewrite with tm_subst_env_open_tm_ty_rec rewrites LHS's tm_subst_env (open_tm_ty_rec ...) into open_tm_ty_rec ...; to match RHS, we need open_tm_ty to be open_tm_ty_rec 0 U — it's definitionally, so reflexivity should work. ✓.

```
Lemma lookup_context_in : forall x Gamma T,
  lookup_context x Gamma = Some T -> In (x, T) Gamma.
Proof.
  intros x Gamma. induction Gamma as [| [y S] Gamma IH]; intros T H; simpl in *.
  - discriminate.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y.
      injection H as HT. subst T. left. reflexivity.
    + right. apply IH. exact H.
Qed.
```

```
Theorem fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall rho sigma1 sigma2 eta1 eta2,
    (forall X, value_rel (rho X)) ->
    (forall X, In X Delta -> lc_ty (sigma1 X)) ->
    (forall X, In X Delta -> lc_ty (sigma2 X)) ->
    (forall y, lc_tm (eta1 y)) ->
    (forall y, lc_tm (eta2 y)) ->
    (forall y S, In (y,S) Gamma -> rel_val S nil rho (eta1 y) (eta2 y)) ->
    erel T nil rho (inst sigma1 eta1 t) (inst sigma2 eta2 t).
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T HT.
  induction HT as [Delta0 Gamma0 x0 T0 Hlook Hwf
                 | L Delta0 Gamma0 T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hpre IHpre
                 | Delta0 Gamma0 t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
                 | L Delta0 Gamma0 t T0 Hpre IHpre
                 | Delta0 Gamma0 t T U Ht IHt HwfU
                 | Delta0 Gamma0 t1 t2 T0 H1 IH1 H2 IH2];
    intros rho sigma1 sigma2 eta1 eta2 Hr Hsig1 Hsig2 Heta1 Heta2 Henv.
```

  has_type [] empty t
    (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) ->
  forall U v,
    wf_ty [] U ->
    value v ->
    has_type [] empty v U ->
    tm_app (tm_tapp t U) v -->* v.
Proof.
  intros t Ht U v HwfU Hv HvU.
  (* rho: singleton relation {(v,v)} *)
  set (rho := fun (_ : atom) (a b : tm) => a = v /\ b = v).
  assert (Hr : forall X, value_rel (rho X)).
  { intros X a b [Ha Hb]. subst a. subst b. split; exact Hv. }
  (* instantiate fundamental *)
  specialize (fundamental [] empty t (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) Ht
               rho (fun X => Ty_FVar X) (fun X => Ty_FVar X)
               (fun x => tm_fvar x) (fun x => tm_fvar x)).
```

Let me summarize the proof approach for the user briefly:
- Binary logical relation on terms indexed by types, de Bruijn type-variable environment (list of relations) plus free type-variable relation environment.
- Value relation at arrows/all with erel (exists a value the term steps to).
- Fundamental theorem: typing derivations map related term environments to related terms, with type substitutions and term substitutions.
- Target: instantiate relation at X as the singleton relation {(v,v)}, use the universal relation at U to get a reduction of t U to a value, then apply the arrow relation at v, yielding a path from app (tapp t U) v to v.

Mention key lemmas: local closure of typed substituted terms, semantic type substitution `rel_val_open_ty` with scoping via `has_type_lc_ty`, and multi-step congruence.
